# Verify-Doc Audit Log

- **2026-09-16** — `NATIVE_FRAME_RATE_WALLCLOCK_RECONCILIATION_GHIDRA_REPORT.md` — CORRECTED — 12 load-bearing claims audited, split by side. CONFIRMED (6, all binary, re-derived not re-read): `Main_Tick` increments `g_CurrentFrameCounter` exactly once per loop, late; the local-skirmish throttle is `speed_byte × 16 ms` (`0x0055E160`, `GetRadarTimer 0x006C8C40` = `timeGetTime() >> 4`); default skirmish speed byte is 1 → cap ≈ 62.5 fps (`0x00697F10`); all gameplay timing is frame-counted (`CDTimerClass 0x0046B640`/`0x00426630`); "15 fps" is an INI/art authoring convention only; byte 0 is uncapped. WRONG/STALE (6, all Rust-side, every one invalidated by `e163a3aa` 2026-07-30 "phase0: implement retail deterministic authority spine" and never revalidated — the corpus was first tracked wholesale by `5aaa9c22` 2026-08-18, which imported 2,544 files without review): (1) §5 table `binary_frame = (total_sim_ms · 15)/1000` at "~21 / s (= sim.tick / 3)" — `binary_frame` now advances once per admitted frame (`world/mod.rs:5836`, `wrapping_add(1)` in `run_late_region`) and the exact-step receipt rejects `frame_delta != 1` — STALE_AFTER_REFACTOR. (2) "One logic *frame* is modelled as **3 sim-ticks**" — no sub-stepping exists; admission is the 16 ms bucket gate in `app/match_runtime/frame_pacer.rs` (`timeGetTime() >> 4` vs `game_speed.clamp(1,6)`), mirroring the native throttle — STALE_AFTER_REFACTOR. (3) "Rust plays the game ~3× slower in wall-clock" — both factors are gone; movement integrates `speed × 1/15` per admitted frame and nothing multiplies by the 22 ms tick — STALE_AFTER_REFACTOR. (4) Combat clause `rof_to_cooldown_ticks` / `GAME_FPS = 15` (`combat/mod.rs:73,2318`) — `GAME_FPS` no longer exists under `src/sim/combat/`; `rof_to_cooldown_frames` (`combat/mod.rs:3367`) consumes ROF frames directly — STALE_AFTER_REFACTOR. (5) `advance_fixed_simulation` (`app_sim_tick.rs:237`) — function and file both absent; now `app/match_runtime/sim_tick.rs` — DEAD_PATH_CITATION. (6) §6 "Option A vs Option B — user decision required" — Option A's shape is what shipped — STALE_AFTER_REFACTOR. NEW RESIDUAL: `render/gpu.rs:184` pins `wgpu::PresentMode::Fifo` and admission is reachable only from `app/frame.rs:142` with no catch-up loop, so achieved logic rate = `min(display refresh, 62.5/s)` — ~4% under native at 60 Hz, never repaid. COVERAGE LIMIT: code-and-binary read only, no wall-clock measurement on either side, so the corrected status is **structurally matched, wall-clock unmeasured** — not parity demonstrated.
